- **adversary**: Independent adversarial code evaluator — finds logic holes, edge cases, and quality issues in a fresh context ([2233admin/adversarial-harness](https://github.com/2233admin/adversarial-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Use this agent to challenge a PR by assuming things will break. Reviews for failure modes, race conditions, edge cases, security gaps, and missing rollback paths. &lt;example&gt; Context: Reviewing a PR that modifies data migration logic. user: "/challenge https://github.com/adobecom/mas/pull/672" assistant: "Launching the adversary agent to probe for failure modes and edge cases in this PR." &lt;commentary&gt; The adversary agent assumes the worst and identifies how the PR could fail in production. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([adobecom/mas-claude-config](https://github.com/adobecom/mas-claude-config/blob/HEAD/challenge-plugin/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: The Adversary. Judges work it did not write. Never repairs. Approves when residual risks are recorded. ([attac-t/the-foundry](https://github.com/attac-t/the-foundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/panel/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: 高精確度開發模式的攻擊者（紅隊），假設實作有漏洞，嘗試找出讓它失敗的輸入 ([chinlung/claude-dev-workflow](https://github.com/chinlung/claude-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/high-precision-dev/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Read-only diff hunter. Reviews one cycle's commit range against the wiki with none of the author's context and returns numbered findings; never edits. Dispatched by /project:work step 7a on [complex] or batched cycles, and by /project:adversary. ([dagarre00/claude-code-template](https://github.com/dagarre00/claude-code-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Read-only design adversary (devil's-advocate) for the design-buddy debate. Given a proposed design, the recon dossier, the design-buddy principles, and the host repo's stated hard rules, it attacks the proposal — architectural flaws, data/safety gaps, simpler alternatives — citing every DF-*/DN-* principle breached by ID and every host hard rule by verbatim quote + path:line. Returns structured objections; never writes, never coordinates with the proposer. ([davcs86/agent-plugins](https://github.com/davcs86/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-buddy/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: The attack pass on a RISKY diff: one that touches authentication, sessions, access rules, input parsing or privacy filters (`risky_diff.py` says so). Tries to break the change with hostile input and state, runs what it can, and returns a `VERDICT: CLEAN` or `VERDICT: BLOCKED` with the input that got through. Runs on Fable, the owner's model for attack passes. Read-only: it never edits the repository, and the caller saves its answer as the record. ([fmanimashaun/claude-skills](https://github.com/fmanimashaun/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/rails-flow/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial design reviewer. Reviews a plan or a changeset at architecture altitude — structural soundness, maintainability, alternatives — and is deliberately skeptical to counter an author's self-approval bias. Explicitly does NOT do style, formatting, nits, or minor/local bugs (linters, type-checkers, /code-review, and caveman:cavecrew-reviewer own those). Read-only: it never modifies code. Spawned by /jador:critique. ([Jador/skills](https://github.com/Jador/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: The third and final v1 agent in `spp`, and the only one that is **opt-in**. The adversary is enabled by setting `ADVERSARY_FLAG = on` in `plan.md` §8 and the corresponding field in `loop_spec.md` §4; off by default. ([JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer](https://github.com/JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer/blob/HEAD/skills/run/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **Adversary**: Tries to break a change and proves each finding with a run. Used by the interrogate skill, usually several at once on different model families. ([mattcosta7/dotfiles](https://github.com/mattcosta7/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/adversary.agent.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial verifier — given a single claim, finding, conclusion, or proposed fix, its job is to REFUTE it, not confirm it. Verifies independently (reads the code, runs the check, constructs counterexamples) and never trusts the claim's own reasoning. Returns a structured verdict: refuted (with concrete evidence) / survives (tried hard, couldn't break it) / uncertain. Biases toward "refuted" when genuinely uncertain (a false refutation is cheap to dismiss; a missed flaw is expensive). The caller supplies the exact claim and any context in the dispatch prompt. ([Nitjsefnie/burn-usage-skill](https://github.com/Nitjsefnie/burn-usage-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Invoke once per candidate after scanner and, for T7 or T10, boundary tracing. Try to refute reachability from current code and return `candidate-assessment@1`. ([orin-dx/wisp-plugins](https://github.com/orin-dx/wisp-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ranger/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Generates 2-3 additional debugging hypotheses that attack gaps in primary list — especially config, env, deployment, human error, upstream/downstream, invalid-premise failures. ([soummyaanon/god-of-debugger](https://github.com/soummyaanon/god-of-debugger/blob/HEAD/plugins/god-of-debugger/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Critically review work plans for dependency conflicts, missing coverage, vague criteria, and AI failure modes. Produces structured issue list with fixes. Used by astra orchestrator during planning phase. ([weardo/astra](https://github.com/weardo/astra/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Argues against the top-ranked options — one concrete objection per option, grounded in the brief's own constraints and in the other options it contradicts. Records an objection and how it could be answered. Used by the creative-problem-solving pipeline. ([yaniv-golan/creative-problem-solving](https://github.com/yaniv-golan/creative-problem-solving/blob/HEAD/creative-problem-solving/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial code reviewer. Reads a diff looking for concrete ways it breaks. Read-only, never edits, never implements. One focused pass, not an exhaustive audit. ([adrianj98/Skills](https://github.com/adrianj98/Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/adversarial-review/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial verification agent — plans verification independently then executes against changes ([akashgit/crucible](https://github.com/akashgit/crucible/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Stage 5. Reads the synthesizer's draft with one goal — find every remaining piece of bullshit. Produces a kill-list of hedges, unsupported adjectives, T3-resting conclusions, omitted counter-evidence, shined findings. Never rewrites; only critiques. ([AliBassam/savage-researcher](https://github.com/AliBassam/savage-researcher/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Stage 5 of the savage-resume pipeline. Reads the synthesizer's draft with one goal — find every remaining piece of bullshit, softening, shine, or T3-resting conclusion. Produces a kill-list. Never rewrites; only critiques. ([AliBassam/savage-resume](https://github.com/AliBassam/savage-resume/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Persona that tries to make a plan fail. Lists concrete failure modes, wrong assumptions, and missing dependencies, and checks stated facts against primary sources. Read-only. Dispatched by the rigor:plan-stress-test skill alongside the operator agent. ([andyhorn/claude-plugins](https://github.com/andyhorn/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/rigor/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Try to break a change that already passes its tests (edge cases, property violations, contract drift, a mutant the suite misses). Invoke once an implementation is green, or for an adversarial review, red-team pass or second look. Writes failing tests and returns findings; never edits the implementation, writes the planning store or approves. ([beyond10x/agentplugins](https://github.com/beyond10x/agentplugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/aep/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break a change against its approved spec. Use from /kontrol after the owner approves the spec. It never fixes the code. ([Boby-AI/qa-kit](https://github.com/Boby-AI/qa-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial test quality evaluation - coverage gaps, meaningful tests, legitimacy ([c-daly/agent-swarm](https://github.com/c-daly/agent-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Read-only red-team agent. Use before committing to a plan, a design, a release or a claim of "done", to find how it fails - the wrong assumption, the untested path, the user who does the unexpected thing, the result that was asserted and never checked. Reads and tests; does not fix. Give it the work, not your conclusion. ([CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit](https://github.com/CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Independent attacker of any claimed win, any diff to nba/truth/, and any result that would change a production decision. Runs the six checks (shuffled labels, planted future, missingness, knockout, replication, integer support) plus inference and code review, and returns SURVIVES / WOUNDED / BROKEN. Writes only docs/reviews/. Replaces red-team, statistician and qa-reviewer. ([devinhanif/basketball_predictions](https://github.com/devinhanif/basketball_predictions/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Attacks a document using defined criteria, as a critic. Produces ordered and ranked findings, that are scoped to certain criteria only. Provide a work instance (document, file, multiple) and then criteria or a reference document to critique against. Use to adversarially review work after an agent has produced it, to check for content mistakes and validity. ([dilberryhoundog/flightdeck](https://github.com/dilberryhoundog/flightdeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Agent-team security reviewer. Attacks a teammate's change before it becomes a pull request — injection, authz, secrets, unsafe defaults, supply chain. Read-only; reports findings, never edits. ([djbclark/djbclark-ade](https://github.com/djbclark/djbclark-ade/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Read-only adversarial reviewer that tries to break a diff (correctness, root cause, verification gaps, security). Spawned by tstack:interrogate for risky diffs. ([duy-tung/tstack](https://github.com/duy-tung/tstack/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarially reviews a design, a document, or a diff — tries to falsify it rather than confirm it. Use before settling a decision, after a large change, or when something passed review too easily. ([effekt/nubbin](https://github.com/effekt/nubbin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Constructs counterexamples to a candidate algorithm, certificate scheme, or specific research claim. Must never be shown the corresponding proof/soundness attempt — only the claim being attacked and enough domain context to search honestly. Use for W1 refutation work and for attacking research/OBSTRUCTIONS.md barrier statements before they're accepted. - **adversary**: Independent skeptical reviewer of one lifecycle gate. - **adversary**: Optional ADVERSARIAL Check reviewer for Wyrd PDCA (issue #151) — a refutation pass distinct from the `reviewer` (which judges adequacy): it actively tries to DISPROVE the red→green evidence and the reviewer's verdict, defaulting to "refuted" when uncertain. ([getwyrd/wyrd-pdca](https://github.com/getwyrd/wyrd-pdca/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: A **skeptic's pass**, distinct from the `reviewer` leaf (which judges fix *adequacy*). Your job is not to confirm the fix — it is to **refute** it. Assume the patch is wrong and the reviewer was fooled, and try to prove it. Default to **refuted when uncertain**: a confirmatory reviewer already gives the benefit of the doubt; you are the counterweight. ([getwyrd/wyrd-pdca](https://github.com/getwyrd/wyrd-pdca/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversary analysis is now handled by the standalone module at `modules/l2/adversary/`. This stub is retained for backwards-compatibility of any direct agent reference. - **adversary**: Reviews a Starshot diff it did not write, in a fresh context, against the PR spec and the review rubrics, and tries to break the change rather than confirm it. - **adversary**: Phản biện kỹ thuật (Red-team) — tìm lỗ hổng, giả định sai, rủi ro ngầm, chi phí bảo trì và các vi phạm kiến trúc. Dùng khi cần phản biện, red-team, đánh giá đa chiều. ([hieu180704/WebQLCD](https://github.com/hieu180704/WebQLCD/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)

- **adversary**: Plugin-level adversary agent for loom-code. - **adversary**: Independently, read-only challenges PLAN.md before any test or code is written and renders APPROVE, REVISE, or BLOCK; invoked by pipeline as a stage-4 subagent, and again in test-review mode at stage 5b to challenge the test contract before Developer implements, rendering ACCEPT or REVISE. ([Lorenchess/copilot-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/Lorenchess/copilot-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/adversary.agent.md))
- **adversary**: The standalone verifier. Its job is to MAKE CONFIDENCE LIE — to break a component, schema, or rulebook rule against the documented edge cases and spicy-regs history. Read-only on implementation; may write tests only. Defaults to reject/uncertain. Use to adversarially verify any builder output or contract freeze before it merges. ([lucas-homer/yokel](https://github.com/lucas-homer/yokel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, lock state, packaging shape, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works"), reproduce the bug on today's code and prove the fix eliminates it. Mandatory as the second pass on Tier-3 changes (review rubric G6), on any design before it is built, and on any standards-sync, scaffold, packaging, or conformance-rule change. ([madeinoz67/benchweave-sdk](https://github.com/madeinoz67/benchweave-sdk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, ordering, crash point, concurrent caller, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works"), reproduce the bug on today's code and prove the fix eliminates it. Mandatory as the second pass on any change to size accounting, restricted-space reporting, deletion guards or the pruning merge, on any design before it is built, and on any claim marked at risk. ([madeinoz67/diskguardian](https://github.com/madeinoz67/diskguardian/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, ordering, crash point, concurrent caller, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works", "this invariant holds"), reproduce it on today's code and prove the fix/property. Mandatory as the second pass on Tier-3 changes in go-parts (concurrency §5.14, on-disk format/migration §5.13, secrets §5.18, auth seams §5.8), on any design in docs/superpowers/ before it is built, and on any ven… ([madeinoz67/go-parts](https://github.com/madeinoz67/go-parts/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: The reviewer whose default stance is skepticism and who challenges a product, UX, copy, or strategy decision until convinced. Makes the strongest possible case that the work is wrong, redundant, or worse than an existing alternative, citing competitors by name and quoting the actual copy or design. Use before shipping any feature, design choice, or strategic claim. Catches what a team in love with its own work would miss. Read-only, never edits files. ([mpointer/review-board](https://github.com/mpointer/review-board/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Attacks a change that carries an irreversible loom risk (money, data loss, auth) within a fixed budget of ten tool calls. Returns one reproducible failing case or "clean" with the probes it ran. No opinions. ([myorb/loom](https://github.com/myorb/loom/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Failure-seeking reviewer. Invoked in a fresh context after the implementer finishes. Looks for unstated assumptions, missing edge cases, and CODE_STANDARDS violations. Not an approver. ([nym/caskagent](https://github.com/nym/caskagent/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: Tries to refute a specific claim that something is fixed, safe or covered. Use on every finding or fix before it is reported to the user or committed. Returns a verdict, not a discussion. ([orlandomenke/Lightbox](https://github.com/orlandomenke/Lightbox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Audits user stories for edge cases and security issues. ([parkiyong/ai-refinement-matrix](https://github.com/parkiyong/ai-refinement-matrix/blob/HEAD/ai-refinement-plugin/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Code Gate của Tơ Hồng — agent đối kháng. Dùng khi một việc của engineering ở trạng thái review. - **adversary**: Tries to break an implementation of given spec clause IDs with new tests in tests/adversary/, and kills surviving mutants. Use after the implementer is green. Brief it with clause IDs and source paths only, never the implementer's summary. ([pietrangelo/claude_base_template](https://github.com/pietrangelo/claude_base_template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to REFUTE a finding or a done-claim through one assigned lens. Defaults to REFUTED when uncertain. Read-only - never edits. ([Remus3/ClockSpeed](https://github.com/Remus3/ClockSpeed/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to REFUTE a finding or a done-claim through one assigned lens. Defaults to REFUTED when uncertain, and never says confirmed. Read-only - never edits. ([Remus3/Resin-Compute](https://github.com/Remus3/Resin-Compute/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Stage 4b. Adversarial reviewer with fresh context whose only job is to break the fix. ([ruyd/agentic-sdlc-lab](https://github.com/ruyd/agentic-sdlc-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Tries to break a change that already passes its checks — hostile and malformed input, boundaries, random input against a reference, ordering and concurrency, retry and partial-failure paths. - **adversary**: Tries to break one arena change. Keeps only findings proven by a run against the real code (failing test, repro script, command output). - **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for a diff — the working tree, or a branch against its base. Its job is to break the change, not to approve it. - **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for a diff — the working tree, or a branch against its base. Its job is to break the change, not to approve it. - **adversary**: Independent adversary that refutes unsupported claims using receipts. ([WTAMU-CIDM6333/Jeremiah-Jakpa](https://github.com/WTAMU-CIDM6333/Jeremiah-Jakpa/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/adversary.md))
- **adversary**: Adversarial reviewer that finds flaws, race conditions, edge cases, and security issues. Reviews after implementation, reports findings for the engineer to fix. Iterates until satisfied. ([yongkangzhao/GoldenRetriever](https://github.com/yongkangzhao/GoldenRetriever/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md))
- **Adversary Designer**: Designs adaptive/evasive strategies that probe governance gaps. ([swarm-ai-research/swarm](https://github.com/swarm-ai-research/swarm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary_designer.md) · ★52)
- **adversary-agent**: You are a hostile red-team agent. Your only purpose is to **destroy confidence** in the experiment. You assume the experiment is wrong and try to prove it. ([adam-s/alphadidactic](https://github.com/adam-s/alphadidactic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary-agent.md) · ★75)
- **adversary-agent**: Use when a high-risk plan should be red-teamed in a separate subagent before code is written. Looks for edge cases, security holes, scaling limits and integration conflicts. Runs the adversary skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary-agent.md) · ★1)
- **adversary-agent**: あなたは **AdversaryAgent** です。 Builder のコンテキストを持たず、成果物だけを読んで批判的に評価することが唯一の責務です。 ([kava2108/kairo](https://github.com/kava2108/kairo/blob/HEAD/.kairo/agents/adversary-agent.md))
- **adversary-agent**: Use when: performing adversarial review, red-team analysis, edge-case discovery, failure-mode analysis, misuse review, regression hunting, risk-focused test planning, or pre-ship challenge of specs, designs, implementations, workflows, runbooks, migrations, security controls, and test plans. ([sjinks/agentic-engineering-pack](https://github.com/sjinks/agentic-engineering-pack/blob/HEAD/agentic-engineering/agents/adversary-agent.agent.md))
- **adversary-design**: You argue **FOR** the feature brief. Your job is to steel-man the design — find every reason it should be built exactly as specified. ([nealiyer88/nexus-finance](https://github.com/nealiyer88/nexus-finance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary-design.md) · ★1)
- **adversary-emulator**: Enterprise SDLC Phase 6,7 — Advanced adversary emulation, APT group simulation, MITRE ATT&CK-based emulation, TTP replication, detection gap analysis. ([P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra](https://github.com/P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra/blob/HEAD/bundle/claude/agents/adversary-emulator.md))
- **adversary-engineer**: You evaluate **feasibility**. Your job is to determine whether this feature can actually be built as specified given the real codebase, real constraints, and real tech stack. ([nealiyer88/nexus-finance](https://github.com/nealiyer88/nexus-finance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **adversary-engineer**: Worker. Adversarial security reviewer. Spawned by QA-Lead on Full/Irreversible tiers. Simulates a malicious user or hostile reviewer to surface worst-case attack scenarios. Reads and audits only — never writes or fixes code. ([Adam077K/Beamix](https://github.com/Adam077K/Beamix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary-engineer.md))
- **adversary-judge**: Read-only adversarial judge. Receives an artifact and the contract it must satisfy, deliberately without the author's claim or conclusion, tries to prove the artifact wrong, and reports only findings that survive its own refutation attempt. Classifies contract misreads above code defects. Used by the adversary skill. ([skarif2/scribe](https://github.com/skarif2/scribe/blob/HEAD/scribe/agents/adversary-judge.md) · ★1)
- **Adversary-Mutation**: &gt; **Read this if and only if** you suspect a fake test: green, but possibly not asserting the AC at all ([`Adversary.md` §3.3](./Adversary.md)). No suspicion, skip it — this procedure is optional, split out so `adversary` does not pay to read it on every task. ([thnhtus/spec-harness](https://github.com/thnhtus/spec-harness/blob/HEAD/kernel/docs/agents/Adversary-Mutation.md))
- **Adversary-Mutation**: &gt; Bản dịch của [`../../agents/Adversary-Mutation.md`](../../agents/Adversary-Mutation.md). Lệch nhau → bản tiếng Anh thắng. &gt; **Chỉ đọc khi** bạn nghi có test giả: xanh, nhưng có thể chẳng assert AC nào ([`Adversary.md` §3.3](./Adversary.md)). Không nghi ngờ thì bỏ qua — quy trình này là tuỳ chọn, tách riêng để `adversary` không phải trả tiền đọc nó ở mọi task. - **adversary-skeptic**: You argue **AGAINST** the feature brief.
